- [ ] **Step 4: The Tracks tab**

```tsx
// src/components/dj/DjTracksTab.tsx
// The Tracks tab: every track the DJ made or remixed, in the Spotify view's
// table (its classes, chips and row actions, Maybe's Yes/No included), newest
// release first, then "Load older releases" when Spotify lists more.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Icon } from '../Icon'
import { SpotifyRowActions } from '../spotify/SpotifyRowActions'
import {
  countByStatus,
  fileName,
  filterRows,
  type SpotifyRow,
  type StatusFilter,
} from '../../lib/spotify/rows'
import { releasedYear } from '../../lib/dj/tracks'
import { refreshFailed, type DjSourceStatus } from '../../lib/dj/page'
import { TRACK_FILTERS, ownedQueue, tracksEmptyText, tracksProgress, tracksTabState } from '../../lib/dj/tabs'
import type { Track } from '../../types/track'
import type { Verdict } from '../../types/spotify'
import type { DjPage } from '../../types/dj'
import '../views/SpotifyView.css'

/** The Spotify view's status chips with their counts; `filters` picks which (the overview card shows three). */
export function StatusChips({
  counts,
  filter,
  onFilter,
  filters = TRACK_FILTERS,
}: {
  counts: Record<StatusFilter, number>
  filter: StatusFilter
  onFilter: (filter: StatusFilter) => void
  filters?: { key: StatusFilter; label: string }[]
}) {
  return (
    <>
      {filters.map(({ key, label }) => (
        <button
          key={key}
          type="button"
          className={`spotify-chip ${filter === key ? 'spotify-chip--on' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => onFilter(key)}
        >
          {label} <small>{counts[key]}</small>
        </button>
      ))}
    </>
  )
}

interface DjTrackRowProps {
  row: SpotifyRow
  /** 1-based, as shown in the # column. */
  number: number
  onVerdict: (row: SpotifyRow, verdict: Verdict) => void
  /** Double-click: plays an Owned row's file. */
  onPlay: (row: SpotifyRow) => void
}

/** One track as the Spotify view draws it — `# · Title · Artist · Released · Status` — plus Maybe's sub-row. */
export function DjTrackRow({ row, number, onVerdict, onPlay }: DjTrackRowProps) {
  return (
    <>
      <div
        className={`spotify-row spotify-row--data ${row.ownership.kind === 'owned' ? 'spotify-row--owned' : ''}`}
        role="row"
        onDoubleClick={() => onPlay(row)}
      >
        <span className="spotify-cell--num">{number}</span>
        <span
          className="spotify-cell--title"
          title={row.track.album ? `${row.track.title} · ${row.track.album}` : row.track.title}
        >
          {row.track.title}
        </span>
        <span className="spotify-cell--artist" title={row.track.artists}>
          {row.track.artists}
        </span>
        <span className="spotify-cell--added">{releasedYear(row.addedAt)}</span>
        {/* Double-clicking a button must not also play the row. */}
        <span className="spotify-cell--status" onDoubleClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <SpotifyRowActions row={row} onVerdict={(verdict) => onVerdict(row, verdict)} />
        </span>
      </div>
      {row.ownership.kind === 'maybe' && row.ownership.file && (
        <div className="spotify-row spotify-row--sub" role="row">
          <span />
          <span className="spotify-hint">
            In library:{' '}
            <code title={row.ownership.file.file_path}>{fileName(row.ownership.file.file_path)}</code>
            <span className="spotify-hint__why">· {row.ownership.reason}</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

/** Grey bars where rows will be, while a first fetch has nothing to show yet. */
function SkeletonRows() {
  return (
    <>
      {[62, 48, 70, 55, 66, 40, 58, 50].map((width, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="spotify-row dj-skeleton" aria-hidden="true">
          <span className="spotify-cell--num">{i + 1}</span>
          <span>
            <i style={{ width: `${width}%` }} />
          </span>
          <span>
            <i style={{ width: `${width - 15}%` }} />
          </span>
          <span>
            <i style={{ width: '60%' }} />
          </span>
          <span />
        </div>
      ))}
    </>
  )
}

interface DjTracksTabProps {
  /** Spotify's connection; null while its status is still being read. */
  connected: boolean | null
  /** The cached page; null until read once. */
  page: DjPage | null
  /** The page's tracks as Spotify rows (`djRows`), newest first. */
  rows: SpotifyRow[]
  /** The page's Spotify refresh. */
  spotify: DjSourceStatus
  progress: { done: number; total: number } | null
  loadingOlder: boolean
  onLoadOlder: () => void
  /** Maybe's Yes / No, stored in spotify_match_verdicts. */
  onVerdict: (row: SpotifyRow, verdict: Verdict) => void
  /** App's player: a library file with a queue. */
  onPlayTrack: (track: Track, queue: Track[], index: number) => void
  /** Settings, with its Spotify section open. */
  onOpenSettings: () => void
}

export function DjTracksTab({
  connected,
  page,
  rows,
  spotify,
  progress,
  loadingOlder,
  onLoadOlder,
  onVerdict,
  onPlayTrack,
  onOpenSettings,
}: DjTracksTabProps) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>('all')
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const counts = useMemo(() => countByStatus(rows), [rows])
  const shown = useMemo(() => filterRows(rows, filter, query), [rows, filter, query])

  const state = tracksTabState({ connected, loaded: page !== null, tracks: rows.length, spotify })
  if (state === 'notConnected') {
    return (
      <div className="dj-connect">
        Connect Spotify to see their tracks
        <button type="button" className="dj-btn" onClick={onOpenSettings}>
          Settings → Spotify
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const playOwned = (row: SpotifyRow) => {
    const start = ownedQueue(
      shown.map((shownRow) => shownRow.ownership),
      row.ownership,
    )
    if (start) onPlayTrack(start.queue[start.index] as Track, start.queue as Track[], start.index)
  }

  const loading = tracksProgress(spotify, progress)
  const failed = refreshFailed(spotify)

  return (
    <div className="dj-tracks">
      <div className="dj-tabhead">
        <span>
          {state === 'list'
            ? `${rows.length} ${rows.length === 1 ? 'track' : 'tracks'} & remixes, newest first`
            : 'Tracks & remixes'}
        </span>
        {loading && <span className="dj-tabhead__busy">· {loading}</span>}
        {failed && (
          <span className="dj-tabhead__failed" title={spotify.error ?? undefined}>
            · couldn&apos;t refresh
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {state === 'list' && (
        <div className="spotify-toolbar dj-toolbar">
          <label className="spotify-search">
            <Icon name="Search" size={14} />
            <input
              type="search"
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search their tracks…"
              spellCheck={false}
            />
          </label>
          <StatusChips counts={counts} filter={filter} onFilter={setFilter} />
        </div>
      )}

      {state === 'empty' ? (
        <p className="dj-note">{tracksEmptyText(spotify, Boolean(page?.profile.spotifyArtistId))}</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="spotify-table dj-table" role="table" aria-label="Tracks and remixes">
          <div className="spotify-row spotify-row--head" role="row">
            <span className="spotify-cell--num">#</span>
            <span>Title</span>
            <span>Artist</span>
            <span>Released</span>
            <span className="spotify-cell--status">Status</span>
          </div>
          {state === 'skeleton' ? (
            <SkeletonRows />
          ) : (
            shown.map((row, index) => (
              <DjTrackRow
                key={row.track.spotifyId}
                row={row}
                number={index + 1}
                onVerdict={onVerdict}
                onPlay={playOwned}
              />
            ))
          )}
          {state === 'list' && shown.length === 0 && <p className="spotify-empty">Nothing matches.</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      {page?.profile.hasOlderReleases && state !== 'skeleton' && (
        <div className="dj-older">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="spotify-mini"
            disabled={loadingOlder || spotify.refreshing}
            onClick={onLoadOlder}
          >
            <Icon name={loadingOlder ? 'Loader' : 'History'} size={12} />
            {loadingOlder ? 'Loading older releases…' : 'Load older releases'}
          </button>
          <span>Older appearances on compilations and other artists&apos; releases</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The Plays tab**

```tsx
// src/components/dj/DjPlaysTab.tsx
// The Plays tab: the named tracks of the DJ's saved sets, most-played first —
// `Track · in N of M sets · Status`, with a bar for the share of their sets.
// Its own status cell: no Yes / No, since verdicts are keyed by Spotify id.
import { openUrl } from '@tauri-apps/plugin-opener'
import { Icon } from '../Icon'
import { CopyButton } from '../spotify/SpotifyRowActions'
import { fileName } from '../../lib/spotify/rows'
import { playSearchUrl, playText, type Play } from '../../lib/dj/plays'
import { inSets, ownedQueue, playShare } from '../../lib/dj/tabs'
import type { Ownership } from '../../lib/spotify/ownership'
import type { Track } from '../../types/track'
import '../views/SpotifyView.css'

/** Owned ✓, or Maybe / Missing with SelectedRecs ↗ and Copy. */
export function PlayStatus({ play, ownership }: { play: Play; ownership: Ownership }) {
  if (ownership.kind === 'owned') {
    return (
      <span className="spotify-status">
        <span className="spotify-status__owned">
          <Icon name="Check" size={14} strokeWidth={2.2} />
          Owned
        </span>
      </span>
    )
  }
  return (
    <span className="spotify-status">
      {ownership.kind === 'maybe' ? (
        <span className="spotify-status__maybe">Maybe</span>
      ) : (
        <span className="spotify-status__missing">Missing</span>
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        className="spotify-mini spotify-mini--icon"
        title="Search on SelectedRecs"
        aria-label="Search on SelectedRecs"
        onClick={() => {
          void openUrl(playSearchUrl(play))
        }}
      >
        <Icon name="ExternalLink" size={12} />
      </button>
      <CopyButton text={playText(play)} />
    </span>
  )
}

/** "4 of 6" and the bar: the share of the DJ's saved sets that name the track. */
export function PlayBar({ count, sets, label }: { count: number; sets: number; label: string }) {
  return (
    <span className="dj-play__sets" title={inSets(count, sets)}>
      <span className="dj-play__bar">
        <i style={{ width: `${playShare(count, sets)}%` }} />
      </span>
      {label}
    </span>
  )
}

interface DjPlayRowProps {
  play: Play
  ownership: Ownership
  /** How many saved sets the DJ has: the M of "in N of M sets". */
  sets: number
  /** 1-based, as shown in the # column. */
  number: number
  /** Double-click: plays an Owned row's file. */
  onPlay: (ownership: Ownership) => void
}

export function DjPlayRow({ play, ownership, sets, number, onPlay }: DjPlayRowProps) {
  const text = playText(play)
  return (
    <>
      <div
        className={`spotify-row spotify-row--data dj-play ${ownership.kind === 'owned' ? 'spotify-row--owned' : ''}`}
        role="row"
        onDoubleClick={() => onPlay(ownership)}
      >
        <span className="spotify-cell--num">{number}</span>
        <span className="spotify-cell--title" title={text}>
          {text}
        </span>
        <PlayBar count={play.count} sets={sets} label={inSets(play.count, sets)} />
        <span className="spotify-cell--status" onDoubleClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <PlayStatus play={play} ownership={ownership} />
        </span>
      </div>
      {ownership.kind === 'maybe' && ownership.file && (
        <div className="spotify-row spotify-row--sub dj-play" role="row">
          <span />
          <span className="spotify-hint">
            In library: <code title={ownership.file.file_path}>{fileName(ownership.file.file_path)}</code>
            <span className="spotify-hint__why">· {ownership.reason}</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

interface DjPlaysTabProps {
  name: string
  /** Their saved sets; null while read. */
  sets: number | null
  /** Most-played first; null while read. */
  plays: Play[] | null
  /** playOwnership per play key, against the shared library index. */
  ownership: Map<string, Ownership>
  /** App's player: a library file with a queue. */
  onPlayTrack: (track: Track, queue: Track[], index: number) => void
}

const MISSING: Ownership = { kind: 'missing' }

export function DjPlaysTab({ name, sets, plays, ownership, onPlayTrack }: DjPlaysTabProps) {
  if (sets === null || plays === null) return <p className="dj-note">Reading your saved sets…</p>
  if (sets === 0) {
    return (
      <p className="dj-note">No saved sets of {name} yet. What they play shows here once you save one.</p>
    )
  }
  if (plays.length === 0) {
    return <p className="dj-note">Their saved sets name no tracks yet — only IDs, or no tracklist.</p>
  }

  const ownershipOf = (play: Play) => ownership.get(play.key) ?? MISSING
  const playOwned = (target: Ownership) => {
    const start = ownedQueue(plays.map(ownershipOf), target)
    if (start) onPlayTrack(start.queue[start.index] as Track, start.queue as Track[], start.index)
  }

  return (
    <div className="dj-plays">
      <div className="dj-tabhead">
        <span>
          What they play · from your {sets} {sets === 1 ? 'set' : 'sets'}, most played first
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="spotify-table dj-table" role="table" aria-label="What they play">
        <div className="spotify-row spotify-row--head dj-play" role="row">
          <span className="spotify-cell--num">#</span>
          <span>Track</span>
          <span>Played</span>
          <span className="spotify-cell--status">Status</span>
        </div>
        {plays.map((play, index) => (
          <DjPlayRow
            key={play.key}
            play={play}
            ownership={ownershipOf(play)}
            sets={sets}
            number={index + 1}
            onPlay={playOwned}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Check by hand**

Run: `npm run tauri dev`.
- **Without Spotify** (Settings → Spotify → Disconnect), open a DJ from a Sets chip. **Tracks** shows only `Connect Spotify to see their tracks` and **Settings → Spotify**; the button opens Settings with the Spotify section expanded. No `Connect` message flashes on a page opened while connected.
- **Plays** works without Spotify: `What they play · from your 6 sets, most played first`, one row per named track (no `ID` rows), the most played first. Each row has a bar as long as its share of the sets and `in 4 of 6 sets`. Owned rows read `✓ Owned` in green; Missing rows read `Missing` with ↗ and **Copy**. **Copy** turns into `✓ Copied` for 1.5 s and the clipboard holds `Artist - Title (Mix)`. ↗ opens SelectedRecs searched for `Artist - Title`. Double-click an Owned row: the file plays.
- **With Spotify connected**, open a DJ never opened before with a large discography: eight grey skeleton rows under `Tracks & remixes · Loading releases…`, then `Loading releases… 20 of 180` counting up while rows appear newest first.
- When it has finished: `214 tracks & remixes, newest first`; chips `All 214  Missing 180  Maybe 11  Owned 23`. Each chip filters the table, and the search box narrows it by title, artist or album. The hero's `You own 23` equals the Owned chip.
- A Maybe row has the `In library: <file> · …` sub-row. **Yes**: it moves to Owned, the chips and the hero update, and the same track reads Owned in Spotify lists that hold it. **No** on another: it becomes Missing (or the next candidate).
- Row actions as in the Spotify view: the green Spotify button plays it in Spotify; ↗ SelectedRecs; Copy. Double-click an Owned row: its file plays.
- With more than 150 appearances (a DJ who remixes a lot), the last row is **Load older releases**: click it — `Loading older releases…`, the header line counts the batches, then older rows are added and the row goes away once Spotify lists no more.
- Make a refresh fail (turn the network off, then open a DJ whose cache is older than a week): the cached rows stay and the header line ends in `· couldn't refresh`. A DJ never opened, with the network off: `Couldn't reach Spotify. The tracks load the next time this page opens.`
- ⋯ → Not this artist? → Spotify → **None**: the tab reads `No Spotify artist by this name. Pick one under ⋯ → Not this artist?`.
- The tables scroll with the rest of the page (no scroll box of their own, unlike the Spotify view).

### Task 17: The Overview, and lineup names that open a DJ page

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/dj/cards.ts` (`CARD_ROWS`, `CARD_TRACK_FILTERS`, `OverviewCounts`, `cardMore`, `cardTarget`, `cardPlacement`, `addCard`, `removeCard`, `lineupNames`, `DjPhoto`, `djPhotos`)
- Test: `src/lib/dj/cards.test.ts`
- Create: `src/components/dj/useOverviewLayout.ts` (`useOverviewLayout` → `{ ids, save }`)
- Create: `src/components/dj/DjOverviewCards.tsx` (`OverviewData`, `OverviewActions`, `OverviewCard`, `EditCard`)
- Create: `src/components/dj/DjOverview.tsx` (`DjOverview`)
- Modify: `src/components/dj/DjGigsTab.tsx` (`GigRow` and `DjGigsTab` take an optional `onOpenDj`: each lineup name is a link)
- Modify: `src/components/dj/DjSetsTab.tsx` (the set card becomes the exported `SetCard`, which the Sets card reuses)
- Modify: `src/components/dj/DjTracksTab.tsx` (`initialFilter`: the Missing card opens the tab on its Missing chip)
- Modify: `src/components/views/DjView.tsx` (the overview placeholder becomes `<DjOverview>`; `onOpenDj` is used at last), `src/components/views/DjView.css`

Task 15 left a placeholder on the Overview tab, with a temporary edit bar. This task builds the overview the spec describes under "Overview and customizing it" (DESIGN.md §12), after the mockup's first section — the normal page on the left, editing on the right.

**The cards.** Every card is a `.dj-card` with the mockup's small upper-case title and an icon. The rows are drawn by the tabs' own components (Tasks 15–16), from values `DjView` already computes once — `trackRows`, `playsOwnership`, the split gigs — so nothing is classified a second time:

| Card | Width | Shows | Title leads to (`cardMore`) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Upcoming gigs | half | the next 3 (`GigRow`); RA's link card / `Looking on Resident Advisor…` / `No gigs listed…` by `gigsState`, as the Gigs tab | Gigs, `all 9 →` |
| What they play | half | the top 4: track, `PlayBar` labelled `4 of 6`, `PlayStatus` | Plays, `from your 6 sets →` |
| Tracks & remixes | full | the newest 3 under its chips **All / Missing / Owned** (`StatusChips` with `CARD_TRACK_FILTERS`), as `DjTrackRow`s with their Yes / No; `Connect Spotify to see their tracks` + **Settings → Spotify** when not connected | Tracks, `all 214 →` |
| Latest sets | full | 3 set cards (`SetCard`, the Sets tab's) | Sets, `all 6 →` |
| Photos | half | the Spotify and the RA photo (`djPhotos`), else one line | — (its tab is `overview`: no link) |
| Past gigs | half | the last 3 | Gigs, `all 20 →` |
| Missing tracks | full | the newest 5 Missing | Tracks **on its Missing chip**, `all 180 missing →` |

The "more" text is left out while its number is unknown or zero; the title itself still leads to the tab. `cardTarget` gives the Missing card a `missing` filter, which `DjView` hands to the Tracks tab as `initialFilter` (read when the tab mounts) — the user lands on what the card showed. Every other way into Tracks opens it on All.

**Normal mode.** A CSS grid with two equal columns. `packOverview` (Task 5) places the stored order: a half card takes the next free half, a full card a row of its own. `cardPlacement` turns each packed `x / y / w` into `gridColumn` / `gridRow`, so the browser never re-flows a card into a gap. Cards keep their natural height (the taller of two half cards sets the row). An overview with every card removed says how to add some.

**Customizing.** The sliders button at the end of the tab row (Task 15) sets `customizing`. `DjOverview` then mounts `OverviewEditor`, whose draft starts from the stored order every time it mounts:
- The indigo bar of the mockup, `Customizing the overview · applies to every DJ page`, with **Reset** and **Done** — full width under the tabs (negative margins take it out of the body's padding). There is no Cancel (spec).
- The cards on `react-grid-layout`, exactly as Home uses it: `WidthProvider(Responsive)` from `'react-grid-layout/legacy'` and its stylesheet, one breakpoint, `cols` 2, `rowHeight` 170, every card `h` 1 and its fixed width, `isResizable` false, `draggableHandle` `.dj-card__grip`, `compactType` `'vertical'`. The grid's own layout is the draft (`onLayoutChange` stores it, as `HomeView` does).
- A card shows the grip, its title and ×; its rows are ghosted (opacity .35) and `inert`, so nothing in them can be clicked while dragging.
- × and the `+ Card` pills under the grid (`Add to overview`, `hiddenCards` in the catalogue's order) read the order off the grid (`orderFromGrid`: by y, then x), change it (`removeCard` / `addCard`, which appends) and re-pack it. **Reset** re-packs `DEFAULT_OVERVIEW`; like every change it is a draft until Done.
- **Done** saves `orderFromGrid` of the current grid. Only that order is stored, so after Done the normal grid packs it again: a half card dragged under another half card in the left column sits beside it afterwards. That is the spec's rule ("the grid is rebuilt by packing the cards in that order").
- Switching to another tab while customizing ends it without saving — leaving the page's Overview is the only way out besides Done.

**Stored once for every DJ page.** `useOverviewLayout` reads `dj_overview_layout` (`OVERVIEW_SETTING`) with `tauriApi.getSetting` when a DJ page mounts, through `parseOverview` (a missing, unreadable or broken value is the default), and `save` writes `serializeOverview` with `tauriApi.setSetting`. It lives in `DjView`, not in the Overview tab, so coming back to Overview from another tab does not re-read it and flash. App remounts `DjView` for every DJ (its motion wrapper is keyed by `dj-<key>`), so a layout saved on one DJ's page is what the next one reads. The state is set in the `.then` callbacks only (react-hooks v7).

**Lineup names open their DJ.** `DjView` finally destructures `onOpenDj`. `GigRow` takes an optional `onOpenDj(name)`: RA's lineup (`Marco Carola, Loco Dice`) is split with `lineupNames` and each name becomes a link-styled button; `DjView` passes `name => onOpenDj(name, null)` to the Gigs tab and to both gig cards. App's `openDj` from a DJ page keeps the first page's origin (Task 12), so Back still returns to where the first page was opened. The DJ is matched on Spotify by name, as from a Sets chip.

```ts
// src/lib/dj/cards.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  CARD_TRACK_FILTERS,
  addCard,
  cardMore,
  cardPlacement,
  cardTarget,
  djPhotos,
  lineupNames,
  removeCard,
  type OverviewCounts,
} from './cards'
import { packOverview } from './overview'

const counts: OverviewCounts = { upcoming: 9, past: 20, sets: 6, tracks: 214, missing: 180 }

describe('the "more" text of a card title', () => {
  it('counts what the tab behind it shows', () => {
    expect(cardMore('upcoming-gigs', counts)).toBe('all 9 →')
    expect(cardMore('past-gigs', counts)).toBe('all 20 →')
    expect(cardMore('plays', counts)).toBe('from your 6 sets →')
    expect(cardMore('tracks', counts)).toBe('all 214 →')
    expect(cardMore('sets', counts)).toBe('all 6 →')
    expect(cardMore('missing', counts)).toBe('all 180 missing →')
  })

  it('says "set" for one', () => {
    expect(cardMore('plays', { ...counts, sets: 1 })).toBe('from your 1 set →')
  })

  it('has none while the number is unknown or zero', () => {
    const none: OverviewCounts = { upcoming: null, past: 0, sets: null, tracks: 0, missing: null }
    for (const id of ['upcoming-gigs', 'past-gigs', 'plays', 'tracks', 'sets', 'missing'] as const) {
      expect(cardMore(id, none)).toBeNull()
    }
  })

  it('has none for Photos, which leads nowhere', () => {
    expect(cardMore('photos', counts)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('where a card title leads', () => {
  it('opens the card’s tab with every row shown', () => {
    expect(cardTarget('upcoming-gigs')).toEqual({ tab: 'gigs', filter: 'all' })
    expect(cardTarget('plays')).toEqual({ tab: 'plays', filter: 'all' })
    expect(cardTarget('tracks')).toEqual({ tab: 'tracks', filter: 'all' })
  })

  it('opens Tracks on its Missing chip from the Missing card', () => {
    expect(cardTarget('missing')).toEqual({ tab: 'tracks', filter: 'missing' })
  })

  it('leads nowhere from Photos', () => {
    expect(cardTarget('photos')).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('a card’s place in the normal two-column grid', () => {
  it('turns the packed position into CSS grid lines', () => {
    const [gigs, plays, tracks, photos] = packOverview(['upcoming-gigs', 'plays', 'tracks', 'photos'])
    expect(cardPlacement(gigs)).toEqual({ gridColumn: '1', gridRow: '1' })
    expect(cardPlacement(plays)).toEqual({ gridColumn: '2', gridRow: '1' })
    expect(cardPlacement(tracks)).toEqual({ gridColumn: '1 / span 2', gridRow: '2' })
    expect(cardPlacement(photos)).toEqual({ gridColumn: '1', gridRow: '3' })
  })
})

describe('adding and removing cards while customizing', () => {
  it('appends a card at the end', () => {
    expect(addCard(['plays', 'tracks'], 'photos')).toEqual(['plays', 'tracks', 'photos'])
  })

  it('never adds a card twice', () => {
    expect(addCard(['plays', 'tracks'], 'plays')).toEqual(['plays', 'tracks'])
  })

  it('removes a card and keeps the others in order', () => {
    expect(removeCard(['upcoming-gigs', 'plays', 'tracks'], 'plays')).toEqual(['upcoming-gigs', 'tracks'])
    expect(removeCard(['tracks'], 'plays')).toEqual(['tracks'])
  })
})

describe('the track filters of the Tracks card', () => {
  it('are the mockup’s All, Missing and Owned', () => {
    expect(CARD_TRACK_FILTERS.map((filter) => filter.key)).toEqual(['all', 'missing', 'owned'])
  })
})

describe('a gig’s lineup as names', () => {
  it('splits RA’s list on commas', () => {
    expect(lineupNames('Marco Carola, Loco Dice')).toEqual(['Marco Carola', 'Loco Dice'])
  })

  it('keeps a single name and drops empty parts', () => {
    expect(lineupNames('Ricardo Villalobos')).toEqual(['Ricardo Villalobos'])
    expect(lineupNames(' Marco Carola ,, Loco Dice, ')).toEqual(['Marco Carola', 'Loco Dice'])
  })

  it('has no names without a lineup', () => {
    expect(lineupNames(null)).toEqual([])
    expect(lineupNames('')).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe('the Photos card', () => {
  it('shows the Spotify photo, then the RA one', () => {
    expect(djPhotos({ spotifyImageUrl: 'https://i.scdn.co/a', raImageUrl: 'https://static.ra.co/b' })).toEqual([
      { url: 'https://i.scdn.co/a', source: 'Spotify' },
      { url: 'https://static.ra.co/b', source: 'Resident Advisor' },
    ])
  })

  it('leaves out what is missing, and a repeat', () => {
    expect(djPhotos({ spotifyImageUrl: null, raImageUrl: 'https://static.ra.co/b' })).toEqual([
      { url: 'https://static.ra.co/b', source: 'Resident Advisor' },
    ])
    expect(djPhotos({ spotifyImageUrl: 'https://x/a', raImageUrl: 'https://x/a' })).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(djPhotos(null)).toEqual([])
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The cards' decisions**

```ts
// src/lib/dj/cards.ts
/**
 * The overview cards' small decisions, kept out of the components so they are
 * tested: what a card title's "all 9 →" says and where it leads, where a card
 * sits in the normal grid, adding and removing cards while customizing, a gig
 * lineup's names (each one a link), and the Photos card's pictures.
 */
import { overviewCard, type DjTab, type GridItem, type OverviewCardId } from './overview'
import { TRACK_FILTERS } from './tabs'
import type { StatusFilter } from '../spotify/rows'
import type { DjProfile } from '../../types/dj'

/** How many rows each card shows (spec: next 3, top 4, newest 3, 3 sets, last 3, newest 5 Missing). */
export const CARD_ROWS = {
  'upcoming-gigs': 3,
  plays: 4,
  tracks: 3,
  sets: 3,
  'past-gigs': 3,
  missing: 5,
} as const

/** The Tracks card's chips: the mockup's All / Missing / Owned (the tab has Maybe too). */
export const CARD_TRACK_FILTERS = TRACK_FILTERS.filter((filter) => filter.key !== 'maybe')

/** What the tabs behind the cards hold; null while unknown. */
export interface OverviewCounts {
  upcoming: number | null
  past: number | null
  /** The DJ's saved sets: the Sets card, and the N of "from your N sets". */
  sets: number | null
  tracks: number | null
  missing: number | null
}

/** "all 9 →" after a card's title; null for Photos, and while the number is unknown or zero. */
export function cardMore(id: OverviewCardId, counts: OverviewCounts): string | null {
  const all = (n: number | null, what = '') => (n ? `all ${n}${what} →` : null)
  switch (id) {
    case 'upcoming-gigs':
      return all(counts.upcoming)
    case 'past-gigs':
      return all(counts.past)
    case 'plays':
      return counts.sets ? `from your ${counts.sets} ${counts.sets === 1 ? 'set' : 'sets'} →` : null
    case 'tracks':
      return all(counts.tracks)
    case 'sets':
      return all(counts.sets)
    case 'missing':
      return all(counts.missing, ' missing')
    case 'photos':
      return null
  }
}

/** Where a card's title leads: its tab, and for Missing the Tracks tab on its Missing chip. Null for Photos. */
export function cardTarget(id: OverviewCardId): { tab: DjTab; filter: StatusFilter } | null {
  const { tab } = overviewCard(id)
  if (tab === 'overview') return null
  return { tab, filter: id === 'missing' ? 'missing' : 'all' }
}

/** A packed card (packOverview) as CSS grid lines: a full card spans both columns. */
export function cardPlacement(item: GridItem): { gridColumn: string; gridRow: string } {
  return {
    gridColumn: item.w === 2 ? '1 / span 2' : String(item.x + 1),
    gridRow: String(item.y + 1),
  }
}

/** "+ Card": appended at the end, never twice. */
export function addCard(ids: OverviewCardId[], id: OverviewCardId): OverviewCardId[] {
  return ids.includes(id) ? ids : [...ids, id]
}

/** "×": the others keep their order. */
export function removeCard(ids: OverviewCardId[], id: OverviewCardId): OverviewCardId[] {
  return ids.filter((other) => other !== id)
}

/** A gig's lineup as RA lists it ("Marco Carola, Loco Dice") → one name per DJ page link. */
export function lineupNames(lineup: string | null): string[] {
  if (!lineup) return []
  return lineup
    .split(',')
    .map((name) => name.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
}

export interface DjPhoto {
  url: string
  source: 'Spotify' | 'Resident Advisor'
}

/** The Photos card: the Spotify artist photo, then the RA one; none twice. */
export function djPhotos(profile: Pick<DjProfile, 'spotifyImageUrl' | 'raImageUrl'> | null): DjPhoto[] {
  if (!profile) return []
  const photos: DjPhoto[] = []
  if (profile.spotifyImageUrl) photos.push({ url: profile.spotifyImageUrl, source: 'Spotify' })
  if (profile.raImageUrl && profile.raImageUrl !== profile.spotifyImageUrl) {
    photos.push({ url: profile.raImageUrl, source: 'Resident Advisor' })
  }
  return photos
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The stored layout**

```ts
// src/components/dj/useOverviewLayout.ts
// The overview's cards, in order: one layout for every DJ page, stored as a
// list of card ids under `dj_overview_layout` in settings. Read once when a
// DJ page opens (so the Overview tab does not flash on every return to it),
// written on Done.
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { tauriApi } from '../../lib/tauri-api'
import {
  OVERVIEW_SETTING,
  parseOverview,
  serializeOverview,
  type OverviewCardId,
} from '../../lib/dj/overview'

export interface OverviewLayout {
  /** The cards in order; null until settings answered. */
  ids: OverviewCardId[] | null
  /** Done: shown at once, stored for every DJ page. */
  save: (ids: OverviewCardId[]) => void
}

export function useOverviewLayout(): OverviewLayout {
  const [ids, setIds] = useState<OverviewCardId[] | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    tauriApi.getSetting(OVERVIEW_SETTING).then(
      (raw) => {
        if (alive) setIds(parseOverview(raw))
      },
      // An unreadable setting is a missing one: the default layout.
      () => {
        if (alive) setIds(parseOverview(null))
      },
    )
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [])

  const save = useCallback((next: OverviewCardId[]) => {
    setIds(next)
    // A failed write keeps the new layout on this page; the next page reads the stored one.
    tauriApi.setSetting(OVERVIEW_SETTING, serializeOverview(next)).catch((error: unknown) => {
      console.error('[DJ] could not save the overview layout', error)
    })
  }, [])

  return { ids, save }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The cards**

```tsx
// src/components/dj/DjOverviewCards.tsx
// The overview's cards (the approved mockup's first section): a title that
// leads to its tab ("all 9 →"), then a few rows drawn by the tabs' own
// components. While customizing, a card has a grip and × instead, and its
// rows show ghosted.
import { useState, type CSSProperties } from 'react'
import { Icon, type IconName } from '../Icon'
import { GigRow, RaLinkCard } from './DjGigsTab'
import { PlayBar, PlayStatus } from './DjPlaysTab'
import { SetCard } from './DjSetsTab'
import { DjTrackRow, StatusChips } from './DjTracksTab'
import { filterRows, type SpotifyRow, type StatusFilter } from '../../lib/spotify/rows'
import { playText, type Play } from '../../lib/dj/plays'
import { ownedQueue, type TracksTabState } from '../../lib/dj/tabs'
import { CARD_ROWS, CARD_TRACK_FILTERS, cardMore, cardTarget, type DjPhoto, type OverviewCounts } from '../../lib/dj/cards'
import { overviewCard, type DjTab, type OverviewCardId } from '../../lib/dj/overview'
import type { GigsState } from '../../lib/dj/page'
import type { Ownership } from '../../lib/spotify/ownership'
import type { DjGig } from '../../types/dj'
import type { Track } from '../../types/track'
import type { Verdict } from '../../types/spotify'
import type { YtSetSummary } from '../../types/youtube'

/** What the cards show — DjView's own memoised values, never classified again here. */
export interface OverviewData {
  /** The DJ's name as the page shows it. */
  name: string
  /** Null until the page and the day are known. */
  gigs: { state: GigsState; upcoming: DjGig[]; past: DjGig[] } | null
  raUrl: string | null
  /** The Tracks tab's state (tracksTabState), shared by the Tracks and Missing cards. */
  tracksState: TracksTabState
  /** Why there are no tracks (tracksEmptyText), for `empty`. */
  tracksEmpty: string
  /** DjView's trackRows: newest first, Owned / Maybe / Missing. */
  rows: SpotifyRow[]
  /** countByStatus(rows), for the Tracks card's chips. */
  trackCounts: Record<StatusFilter, number>
  /** Null while read. */
  sets: YtSetSummary[] | null
  /** Most played first; null while read. */
  plays: Play[] | null
  /** DjView's playsOwnership. */
  playsOwnership: Map<string, Ownership>
  photos: DjPhoto[]
}

export interface OverviewActions {
  onVerdict: (row: SpotifyRow, verdict: Verdict) => void
  onPlayTrack: (track: Track, queue: Track[], index: number) => void
  onOpenSettings: () => void
  onOpenSet: (videoId: string) => void
  /** A lineup name: that DJ's page. */
  onOpenDj: (name: string) => void
}

const CARD_ICONS: Record<OverviewCardId, IconName> = {
  'upcoming-gigs': 'CalendarDays',
  plays: 'ListMusic',
  tracks: 'Music',
  sets: 'Radio',
  photos: 'Image',
  'past-gigs': 'History',
  missing: 'CircleDashed',
}

const MISSING: Ownership = { kind: 'missing' }

function Note({ children }: { children: string }) {
  return <p className="dj-note">{children}</p>
}

function GigsBody({ kind, data, actions }: { kind: 'upcoming' | 'past'; data: OverviewData; actions: OverviewActions }) {
  if (!data.gigs) return <Note>Reading the gigs…</Note>
  const { state, upcoming, past } = data.gigs
  // Design decision 8: RA failed or has no such artist, and nothing is cached.
  if (state === 'link') return <RaLinkCard raUrl={data.raUrl} />
  if (state === 'searching') return <Note>Looking on Resident Advisor…</Note>
  if (state === 'none') return <Note>No gigs listed on Resident Advisor.</Note>
  const shown = kind === 'upcoming' ? upcoming.slice(0, CARD_ROWS['upcoming-gigs']) : past.slice(0, CARD_ROWS['past-gigs'])
  if (shown.length === 0) return <Note>{kind === 'upcoming' ? 'No upcoming gigs listed.' : 'No past gigs listed.'}</Note>
  return (
    <div className="dj-gigs">
      {shown.map((gig) => (
        <GigRow key={gig.raEventId} gig={gig} onOpenDj={actions.onOpenDj} />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function PlaysBody({ data }: { data: OverviewData }) {
  if (data.sets === null || data.plays === null) return <Note>Reading your saved sets…</Note>
  const sets = data.sets.length
  if (sets === 0) return <Note>{`No saved sets of ${data.name} yet.`}</Note>
  if (data.plays.length === 0) return <Note>Their saved sets name no tracks yet.</Note>
  return (
    <div className="dj-card__plays">
      {data.plays.slice(0, CARD_ROWS.plays).map((play) => {
        const text = playText(play)
        return (
          <div key={play.key} className="dj-card-play">
            <span className="dj-card-play__text" title={text}>
              {text}
            </span>
            <PlayBar count={play.count} sets={sets} label={`${play.count} of ${sets}`} />
            <PlayStatus play={play} ownership={data.playsOwnership.get(play.key) ?? MISSING} />
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

/** The Tracks card (newest 3 under its chip) and the Missing card (newest 5 Missing). */
function TracksBody({
  filter,
  limit,
  data,
  actions,
}: {
  filter: StatusFilter
  limit: number
  data: OverviewData
  actions: OverviewActions
}) {
  if (data.tracksState === 'notConnected') {
    return (
      <div className="dj-connect">
        Connect Spotify to see their tracks
        <button type="button" className="dj-btn" onClick={actions.onOpenSettings}>
          Settings → Spotify
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }
  if (data.tracksState === 'skeleton') return <Note>Loading their tracks…</Note>
  if (data.tracksState === 'empty') return <Note>{data.tracksEmpty}</Note>
  const shown = filterRows(data.rows, filter, '').slice(0, limit)
  if (shown.length === 0) {
    return <Note>{filter === 'owned' ? 'You own none of their tracks yet.' : 'Nothing missing.'}</Note>
  }
  // Double-click an Owned row: its file plays, queued with the card's other Owned rows.
  const playOwned = (row: SpotifyRow) => {
    const start = ownedQueue(
      shown.map((shownRow) => shownRow.ownership),
      row.ownership,
    )
    if (start) actions.onPlayTrack(start.queue[start.index] as Track, start.queue as Track[], start.index)
  }
  return (
    <div className="spotify-table dj-table dj-card__table" role="table">
      {shown.map((row, index) => (
        <DjTrackRow
          key={row.track.spotifyId}
          row={row}
          number={index + 1}
          onVerdict={actions.onVerdict}
          onPlay={playOwned}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function SetsBody({ data, actions }: { data: OverviewData; actions: OverviewActions }) {
  if (data.sets === null) return <Note>Reading your saved sets…</Note>
  if (data.sets.length === 0) return <Note>{`No saved sets of ${data.name} yet.`}</Note>
  return (
    <div className="dj-sets dj-sets--card">
      {data.sets.slice(0, CARD_ROWS.sets).map((set) => (
        <SetCard key={set.video_id} set={set} onOpen={actions.onOpenSet} />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function PhotosBody({ data }: { data: OverviewData }) {
  if (data.photos.length === 0) return <Note>Neither Spotify nor Resident Advisor has a photo of them.</Note>
  return (
    <div className="dj-photos">
      {data.photos.map((photo) => (
        <figure key={photo.url} className="dj-photo">
          <img src={photo.url} alt={`${data.name} on ${photo.source}`} loading="lazy" />
          <figcaption>{photo.source}</figcaption>
        </figure>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function CardBody({
  id,
  filter,
  data,
  actions,
}: {
  id: OverviewCardId
  /** The Tracks card's chip. */
  filter: StatusFilter
  data: OverviewData
  actions: OverviewActions
}) {
  switch (id) {
    case 'upcoming-gigs':
      return <GigsBody kind="upcoming" data={data} actions={actions} />
    case 'past-gigs':
      return <GigsBody kind="past" data={data} actions={actions} />
    case 'plays':
      return <PlaysBody data={data} />
    case 'tracks':
      return <TracksBody filter={filter} limit={CARD_ROWS.tracks} data={data} actions={actions} />
    case 'missing':
      return <TracksBody filter="missing" limit={CARD_ROWS.missing} data={data} actions={actions} />
    case 'sets':
      return <SetsBody data={data} actions={actions} />
    case 'photos':
      return <PhotosBody data={data} />
  }
}

interface OverviewCardProps {
  id: OverviewCardId
  data: OverviewData
  actions: OverviewActions
  counts: OverviewCounts
  /** A title was clicked: its tab, on that chip for Tracks. */
  onOpenTab: (tab: DjTab, filter: StatusFilter) => void
  /** Its place in the two-column grid (cardPlacement). */
  style: CSSProperties
}

/** A card as the overview shows it normally: the title leads to its tab. */
export function OverviewCard({ id, data, actions, counts, onOpenTab, style }: OverviewCardProps) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>('all')
  const card = overviewCard(id)
  const target = cardTarget(id)
  const more = cardMore(id, counts)
  const open = target ? () => onOpenTab(target.tab, target.filter) : null
  return (
    <section className="dj-card" style={style} aria-label={card.title}>
      <h4 className="dj-card__head">
        <Icon name={CARD_ICONS[id]} size={14} />
        {open ? (
          <button type="button" className="dj-card__title dj-card__title--link" onClick={open}>
            {card.title}
          </button>
        ) : (
          <span className="dj-card__title">{card.title}</span>
        )}
        {id === 'tracks' && data.tracksState === 'list' && (
          <span className="dj-card__chips">
            <StatusChips
              counts={data.trackCounts}
              filter={filter}
              onFilter={setFilter}
              filters={CARD_TRACK_FILTERS}
            />
          </span>
        )}
        {open && more && (
          <button type="button" className="dj-card__more" onClick={open}>
            {more}
          </button>
        )}
      </h4>
      <CardBody id={id} filter={filter} data={data} actions={actions} />
    </section>
  )
}

interface EditCardProps {
  id: OverviewCardId
  data: OverviewData
  actions: OverviewActions
  onRemove: (id: OverviewCardId) => void
}

/** A card while customizing: dragged by its grip, removed with ×, its rows ghosted and inert. */
export function EditCard({ id, data, actions, onRemove }: EditCardProps) {
  const card = overviewCard(id)
  return (
    <section className="dj-card dj-card--edit" aria-label={card.title}>
      <h4 className="dj-card__head">
        <span className="dj-card__grip" title="Drag to move">
          <Icon name="GripVertical" size={14} />
        </span>
        <Icon name={CARD_ICONS[id]} size={14} />
        <span className="dj-card__title">{card.title}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="dj-card__remove"
          title={`Remove ${card.title}`}
          aria-label={`Remove ${card.title}`}
          onClick={() => onRemove(id)}
        >
          <Icon name="X" size={14} />
        </button>
      </h4>
      <div className="dj-card__ghost" aria-hidden="true" inert>
        <CardBody id={id} filter="all" data={data} actions={actions} />
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The overview and its editing**

```tsx
// src/components/dj/DjOverview.tsx
// The Overview tab: the chosen cards in the chosen order, one layout for every
// DJ page (spec "Overview and customizing it"). Normally a CSS grid with each
// card at its packed place and natural height; while customizing, the
// indigo bar, react-grid-layout (as on Home) with one row height for every
// card, and "+ Card" pills for the cards not shown.
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Responsive, WidthProvider } from 'react-grid-layout/legacy'
import type { Layout, LayoutItem } from 'react-grid-layout/legacy'
import 'react-grid-layout/css/styles.css'
import { Icon } from '../Icon'
import { EditCard, OverviewCard, type OverviewActions, type OverviewData } from './DjOverviewCards'
import { addCard, cardPlacement, removeCard, type OverviewCounts } from '../../lib/dj/cards'
import {
  DEFAULT_OVERVIEW,
  hiddenCards,
  orderFromGrid,
  packOverview,
  type DjTab,
  type OverviewCardId,
} from '../../lib/dj/overview'
import type { StatusFilter } from '../../lib/spotify/rows'

const ResponsiveGridLayout = WidthProvider(Responsive)

interface DjOverviewProps {
  /** The stored cards in order (useOverviewLayout); null until read. */
  ids: OverviewCardId[] | null
  /** The sliders button switched editing on. */
  customizing: boolean
  /** Done: the order read off the grid. */
  onDone: (ids: OverviewCardId[]) => void
  data: OverviewData
  actions: OverviewActions
  /** A card title: its tab (Tracks on the Missing chip from the Missing card). */
  onOpenTab: (tab: DjTab, filter: StatusFilter) => void
}

export function DjOverview({ ids, customizing, onDone, data, actions, onOpenTab }: DjOverviewProps) {
  if (ids === null) return null
  if (customizing) return <OverviewEditor initial={ids} data={data} actions={actions} onDone={onDone} />
  if (ids.length === 0) {
    return <p className="dj-note">No cards on the overview. Add some with the sliders button at the end of the tabs.</p>
  }

  const listed = data.tracksState === 'list'
  const counts: OverviewCounts = {
    upcoming: data.gigs?.upcoming.length ?? null,
    past: data.gigs?.past.length ?? null,
    sets: data.sets?.length ?? null,
    tracks: listed ? data.trackCounts.all : null,
    missing: listed ? data.trackCounts.missing : null,
  }
  return (
    <div className="dj-overview">
      {packOverview(ids).map((item) => (
        <OverviewCard
          key={item.i}
          id={item.i}
          data={data}
          actions={actions}
          counts={counts}
          onOpenTab={onOpenTab}
          style={cardPlacement(item)}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

interface OverviewEditorProps {
  /** The stored order when editing began. */
  initial: OverviewCardId[]
  data: OverviewData
  actions: OverviewActions
  onDone: (ids: OverviewCardId[]) => void
}

/**
 * Mounted while customizing, so its draft starts from the stored order each
 * time. The grid's own layout is the draft: after a drag the order is read
 * off it (y, then x), and × / + / Reset re-pack that order. No Cancel (spec).
 */
function OverviewEditor({ initial, data, actions, onDone }: OverviewEditorProps) {
  const [grid, setGrid] = useState<LayoutItem[]>(() => packOverview(initial))
  const order = orderFromGrid(grid)
  const repack = (next: OverviewCardId[]) => setGrid(packOverview(next))
  const hidden = hiddenCards(order)

  return (
    <>
      <div className="dj-editbar">
        <Icon name="SlidersHorizontal" size={14} />
        Customizing the overview · applies to every DJ page
        <span className="dj-editbar__gap" />
        <button type="button" className="dj-btn" onClick={() => repack(DEFAULT_OVERVIEW)}>
          Reset
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="dj-btn dj-btn--primary" onClick={() => onDone(order)}>
          Done
        </button>
      </div>

      {order.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="dj-note">No cards on the overview. Add some below.</p>
      ) : (
        <ResponsiveGridLayout
          className="dj-overview-grid"
          layouts={{ lg: grid }}
          breakpoints={{ lg: 0 }}
          cols={{ lg: 2 }}
          rowHeight={170}
          margin={[16, 16]}
          containerPadding={[0, 0]}
          isDraggable
          isResizable={false}
          draggableHandle=".dj-card__grip"
          compactType="vertical"
          onLayoutChange={(layout: Layout) => setGrid([...layout])}
        >
          {order.map((id) => (
            <div key={id}>
              <EditCard id={id} data={data} actions={actions} onRemove={(card) => repack(removeCard(order, card))} />
            </div>
          ))}
        </ResponsiveGridLayout>
      )}

      {hidden.length > 0 && (
        <div className="dj-addc">
          <b>
            <Icon name="Plus" size={14} />
            Add to overview
          </b>
          <div className="dj-addc__pills">
            {hidden.map((card) => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={card.id}
                className="dj-pill"
                onClick={() => repack(addCard(order, card.id))}
              >
                <Icon name="Plus" size={12} />
                {card.title}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
```

```css

/* ---- Overview: cards in two columns (packOverview), natural height ---- */

.dj-overview {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 16px;
}

.dj-card {
  box-sizing: border-box;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--bg-secondary);
}

.dj-card__head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  margin: 0 0 8px;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.dj-card__title {
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font: inherit;
  letter-spacing: inherit;
  text-transform: inherit;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.dj-card__title--link,
.dj-card__more {
  cursor: pointer;
}

.dj-card__title--link:hover {
  color: var(--text-primary);
}

.dj-card__more {
  margin-left: auto;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  background: none;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 500;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.dj-card__more:hover {
  color: var(--text-primary);
}

/* The mockup's smaller chips, after the Tracks card's title. */
.dj-card__chips {
  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-left: 6px;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  text-transform: none;
}

.dj-card__chips .spotify-chip {
  height: 24px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  font-size: 11px;
}

/* A card's rows have no header row: no line above the first. */
.dj-card__table {
  border-top: 0;
}

/* What they play: Track · bar + "4 of 6" · Status. */
.dj-card-play {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto auto;
  align-items: center;
  height: 32px;
  font-size: 13px;
}

.dj-card-play__text {
  overflow: hidden;
  padding-right: 10px;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.dj-sets--card {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
}

.dj-photos {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(140px, 1fr));
  gap: 8px;
}

.dj-photo {
  position: relative;
  margin: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--bg-tertiary);
  aspect-ratio: 1;
}

.dj-photo img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}

.dj-photo figcaption {
  position: absolute;
  right: 8px;
  bottom: 6px;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
  font-size: 10px;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
  text-shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
}

/* A lineup name: a link to that DJ's page. */
.dj-gig__dj {
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font: inherit;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.dj-gig__dj:hover {
  color: var(--text-primary);
  text-decoration: underline;
}

/* ---- Customizing the overview ---- */

/* Full width under the tabs, as in the mockup: out of the body's padding. */
.dj-editbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  margin: -16px -24px 0;
  padding: 8px 24px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(var(--accent-rgb), 0.35);
  background: rgba(var(--accent-rgb), 0.12);
  color: #c7d2fe;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.dj-editbar__gap {
  flex: 1;
}

.dj-editbar .dj-btn {
  height: 26px;
}

.dj-overview-grid {
  min-width: 0;
}

.dj-overview-grid .react-grid-placeholder {
  border: 2px dashed var(--accent);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: rgba(var(--accent-rgb), 0.15);
  opacity: 1;
}

.dj-card--edit {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px dashed #4b4b6b;
}

.dj-card__grip {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  cursor: grab;
}

.dj-card__grip:active {
  cursor: grabbing;
}

.dj-card__remove {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  margin-left: auto;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: none;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.dj-card__remove:hover {
  background: var(--bg-tertiary);
  color: var(--text-primary);
}

/* The rows, ghosted: they show what the card is, nothing in them reacts. */
.dj-card__ghost {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  opacity: 0.35;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.dj-addc {
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border: 1px dashed var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  color: var(--text-secondary);
}

.dj-addc b {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-size: 12px;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.dj-addc__pills {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
}

.dj-pill {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  height: 26px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 13px;
  background: var(--bg-tertiary);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  font-size: 12px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.dj-pill:hover {
  background: var(--bg-elevated);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Check by hand**

Run: `npm run tauri dev`. Use a DJ with saved sets, upcoming gigs on RA and tracks on Spotify (e.g. open Search, type `luciano`, click the card).
- **Default overview:** Upcoming gigs (left) and What they play (right) side by side, Tracks & remixes across the full width below. Titles read `UPCOMING GIGS … all 9 →`, `WHAT THEY PLAY … from your 6 sets →`, `TRACKS & REMIXES  All 214  Missing 180  Owned 23 … all 214 →`. Upcoming gigs lists the next 3; Plays the top 4 with bars and `4 of 6`; Tracks the newest 3. The chips switch the 3 rows (Owned shows the newest 3 Owned). Clicking a title or its `all … →` opens that tab.
- **Customize:** click the sliders icon at the end of the tab row. The indigo bar `Customizing the overview · applies to every DJ page` with **Reset** and **Done** spans the page under the tabs; the cards get a dashed border, a grip, × and ghosted rows (nothing in them reacts); `ADD TO OVERVIEW` lists `+ Latest sets`, `+ Photos`, `+ Past gigs`, `+ Missing tracks`.
- Click `+ Photos` and `+ Latest sets`: they are added at the end and leave the pills. Click × on What they play: it goes, and comes back as a pill. Drag Photos by its grip above Upcoming gigs. Click **Done**: the bar goes, and the overview reads Photos, Upcoming gigs (side by side), Tracks & remixes, Latest sets. Photos shows the Spotify and the RA picture, each captioned; Latest sets shows 3 set cards, and a click opens the set in Sets.
- **Every DJ page:** open another DJ (Search, or a lineup name below) — same cards, same order. Quit the app and start it again, open any DJ — same layout.
- **Reset:** sliders → **Reset** → the default three cards come back in the grid; nothing is saved until **Done**. Click Done: the default is stored.
- Sliders, drag a card, then click the **Tracks** tab instead of Done: editing ends and the overview is as it was.
- Add `+ Missing tracks`, Done: the newest 5 Missing; `all 180 missing →` opens Tracks on the **Missing** chip. Click the Tracks tab itself: it opens on All.
- **Without Spotify** (Settings → Spotify → Disconnect): the Tracks and Missing cards show `Connect Spotify to see their tracks` with **Settings → Spotify**; the gig, Plays and Sets cards work as before.
- **A DJ RA does not know** (or with the network off and nothing cached): the Upcoming gigs card shows only `Gigs on Resident Advisor ↗`.
- **Lineup links:** on Upcoming gigs (card or Gigs tab) a gig reads `w/ Marco Carola, Loco Dice`, each name underlined on hover. Click `Loco Dice`: Loco Dice's page opens (Overview tab). Click **Back**: you are where the first DJ page was opened (Search with its query, or the set in Sets), not on Luciano. Past gigs' names work the same.

- [ ] **Step 2: By hand, without a Spotify account**

This step uses a made-up DJ, **Lucio Zzyzx**, so the numbers cannot mix with your real saved sets, and neither Spotify nor Resident Advisor knows him. He has three saved sets, one of them back to back with **Marta Zzyzx**. A watched DJ, **Lucia Zzyzx**, has no sets yet. The real Luciano comes in Step 3.

1. **Launch once, so migration 016 runs.**
   - Launch with `npm run tauri dev`, wait for the window, then quit (`⌘Q`).
   - Check the database and the library:

```bash
DB="$HOME/Library/Application Support/com.nemanjamarjanovic.recodeck/recodeck.db"
sqlite3 "$DB" ".tables" | tr -s ' ' '\n' | grep '^dj_'
sqlite3 "$DB" "SELECT artist, title FROM tracks WHERE artist <> '' AND title <> ''
  AND artist NOT IN ('Unknown Artist', 'Unknown', 'Various Artists', 'Various', 'VA') LIMIT 1"
```

   - The first command prints `dj_gigs`, `dj_profiles`, `dj_spotify_releases` and `dj_spotify_tracks`. The identifier comes from `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` (`com.nemanjamarjanovic.recodeck`).
   - The second prints one library file, `<artist>|<title>`. This is the **owned track** below: each seeded set plays it, so Plays shows it as Owned.
2. **Back up and seed.** The app must be closed for this.

```bash
cp "$DB" "$DB.before-dj-check"
sqlite3 "$DB" <<'SQL'
-- No Spotify account during this step. The backup keeps yours.
DELETE FROM settings WHERE key IN ('spotify_refresh_token', 'spotify_account_name', 'spotify_needs_reconnect');
-- The overview starts from its default cards.
DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = 'dj_overview_layout';

-- The library file the seeded sets play (Owned on the Plays tab).
CREATE TEMP TABLE own AS
  SELECT artist, title FROM tracks
  WHERE artist <> '' AND title <> ''
    AND artist NOT IN ('Unknown Artist', 'Unknown', 'Various Artists', 'Various', 'VA')
  LIMIT 1;

-- Three saved sets of Lucio Zzyzx, one of them b2b with Marta Zzyzx. The

-- so they come out the same. One exception: when the owned file's title
-- carries a mix in brackets, the parser splits that mix off.
DELETE FROM yt_tracks WHERE video_id IN ('djchkLZ0001', 'djchkLZ0002', 'djchkLZ0003');
INSERT OR REPLACE INTO yt_sets
  (video_id, url, title, channel, published_at, duration_ms, fetched_at,
   status, confidence, source_count, track_count, raw_json, added_at)
SELECT s.id, 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=' || s.id, s.title, s.channel,
       '2025-08-01T00:00:00Z', 6720000, '2026-10-03T10:00:00Z', 'ok', 1.0, 1, 8,
       json_object(
         'video', json_object(
           'id', s.id,
           'url', 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=' || s.id,
           'title', s.title,
           'channel', s.channel,
           'publishedAt', '2025-08-01T00:00:00Z',
           'description', replace(s.lines, '{own}', own.artist || ' - ' || own.title),
           'durationMs', 6720000),
         'comments', json_array(),
         'fetchedAt', '2026-10-03T10:00:00Z'),
       datetime('now')
```

```bash

sudo dscacheutil -flushcache; sudo killall -HUP mDNSResponder
curl -sS -m 5 -o /dev/null https://ra.co/graphql || echo "ra.co blocked"
```

      The last line prints a connection error and `ra.co blocked`. (Turning Wi-Fi off works too, but it blocks the Overview's thumbnails as well.)

      - Plays, Sets and the hero are unchanged;
      - `sqlite3 "$DB" "SELECT ra_synced_at FROM dj_profiles WHERE name_key = 'lucio zzyzx'"` still prints an empty value. A failed refresh writes nothing.
    - Click **Marco Carola** in the Amnesia gig's lineup. His Gigs tab, and his Upcoming gigs card, show only **Gigs on Resident Advisor ↗**, opening `https://ra.co/dj/marcocarola`. There is no number on the Gigs tab, and no "next gig" in the hero.
    - **No cached gigs.** Quit, then delete Lucio's gigs:

```bash
sqlite3 "$DB" "DELETE FROM dj_gigs WHERE name_key = 'lucio zzyzx'"
```

      Launch and open Lucio:
      - the Gigs tab and the Upcoming gigs and Past gigs cards show only **Gigs on Resident Advisor ↗**, opening `https://ra.co/dj/luciozzyzx`;
      - the rest is intact: Plays 18, Sets 3, the hero's `3 sets saved` (with no next gig), Watch for sets, the layout.
    - Quit and **unblock**:

```bash
sudo sed -i '' '/^127\.0\.0\.1 ra\.co$/d' /etc/hosts
sudo dscacheutil -flushcache; sudo killall -HUP mDNSResponder
curl -sS -m 10 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://ra.co/
```

      The last command prints an HTTP status, not a connection error.
    - Launch, open Lucio, then Marco Carola from the lineup:
      - "Looking on Resident Advisor…" shows briefly;
      - then his real upcoming gigs, with the Gigs number, and his RA button opening `https://ra.co/dj/marcocarola`.
      - The block was the only reason for the link card.
16. **Clean up.**
    - Quit, then `mv "$DB.before-dj-check" "$DB"`. This also brings back your Spotify account settings, and removes every seeded row and the DJ pages opened in this step.
    - `grep -c 'ra\.co' /etc/hosts` prints `0`.

- [ ] **Step 3: On the real account (needs the user)**

The account connected in the Spotify plan is back after Step 2's restore. Settings → Spotify shows "Connected as <name>".

1. **Search.** Type `luciano`.
   - The known card comes first, `N sets` when you have saved Luciano sets. Its photo is the Spotify artist of exactly that name.
   - After a short pause (300 ms), the `on Spotify` cards follow:
     - at most **6**, never 7 or more;
     - none with the known card's name;
     - each with Spotify's photo.
   - Photos load: no CSP errors in the devtools console (right-click → Inspect → Console). Task 13 added `i.scdn.co`, `*.spotifycdn.com`, `static.ra.co` and `images.ra.co` to `img-src`.
2. **First open of a large discography.** Click the known **Luciano** card.
   - The hero:
     - his Spotify photo;
     - genres only if Spotify sends any;
     - **Spotify**, which opens `https://open.spotify.com/artist/<id>`, and **RA**, which opens his RA page.
   - The **Tracks** tab:
     - first `Tracks & remixes · Loading releases… 20 of <Y>` over grey skeleton rows;
     - then `<N> tracks & remixes, newest first · Loading releases… <X> of <Y>`, with rows appearing after every batch of 20 releases and the **Tracks** number growing;
     - when the fetch ends, the progress goes.
   - The other tabs work meanwhile.
   - `sqlite3 "$DB" "SELECT spotify_synced_at, spotify_appears_limit, spotify_appears_total FROM dj_profiles WHERE name_key = 'luciano'"` shows a time, `150` and Spotify's total.
   - Spot-check:
     - ten rows are really his tracks or remixes. On a compilation, only the tracks he is credited on;
     - one track found on several releases shows once;
     - ten Owned rows really are in the library, and ten Missing rows really are not;
     - the Maybe sub-rows name plausible files.
3. **Load older releases.** When Spotify lists more than 150 appearances, the table ends with **Load older releases** and "Older appearances on compilations and other artists' releases".
   - Click it: it reads **Loading older releases…**, the progress line runs again, and older rows join the list.
   - `spotify_appears_limit` is now `300`.
   - The row stays only while Spotify lists still more.
4. **Reopen within 7 days.** Back, then Luciano again:
   - rows at once, with no "Loading releases…" (the refresh answers `fresh`);
   - `spotify_synced_at` and `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dj_spotify_tracks WHERE name_key = 'luciano'` are unchanged.
5. **Hero, Gigs, and ra.co.**
   - The meta line reads `You own <N> of <M> tracks · <K> sets saved · next gig <date>`.
   - Open `https://ra.co/dj/luciano`:
     - the Gigs tab's upcoming gigs match RA's list (dates, venues, cities), and the tab's number is that count;
     - **Past gigs** are RA's latest past events, latest first.
   - Lineup names open those DJs' pages, and Back returns to Search.
6. **A Spotify card.** Back to Search, `luciano`, and open one `on Spotify` card. The page uses exactly that artist:
   - its photo;
   - its **Spotify** button;
   - a first fetch with progress.
7. **From a KEEZY set.**
   - Sets → Library, open a KEEZY set: the chip's **KEEZY** is a link to his page, and Sets stays lit.
   - **Back** returns to that set, open.
8. **Not this artist?** Do this on KEEZY's page or another small discography, since each change refetches.
   - **⋯** shows:
     - "Searching Spotify and Resident Advisor…";
     - a Spotify list with the current artist checked, plus **None**;
     - a Resident Advisor list with the current one checked, plus **None**.
   - Pick another Spotify artist:
     - the rows clear at once;
     - "Loading releases…" fetches that artist's tracks;
     - the Spotify button opens the new artist.
   - Pick Spotify's **None**:
     - the rows clear, and the Tracks tab reads "No Spotify artist by this name. Pick one under ⋯ → Not this artist?";
     - the hero has no Spotify button;
     - reopening the page stays at None. A manual choice is never replaced automatically.
   - Pick the right artist again: a fresh first fetch.
   - Resident Advisor's **None** leaves only **Gigs on Resident Advisor ↗**. Picking the right RA artist brings the gigs back.
9. **Maybe, Yes / No.** On a page with Maybe rows, chip **Maybe**:
   - **Yes** turns the row Owned, and the hero's "You own" rises by one. It stays Owned after a restart, and in the Spotify section too, since the verdict is shared.
   - **No** on another Maybe row turns it Missing, also after a restart.
10. **"you own N" on Search.** Type `luciano`: his card reads `<K> sets · you own <N>`, with the same N as his hero.
11. **Disconnect.** Settings → Spotify → **Disconnect** (confirm if asked). Then:

```bash
sqlite3 "$DB" "SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dj_spotify_tracks), (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dj_spotify_releases),
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dj_profiles WHERE spotify_artist_id IS NOT NULL OR spotify_manual = 1 OR spotify_synced_at IS NOT NULL),
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dj_gigs)"
```

    This prints `0|0|0|<more than 0>`. Every DJ's Spotify side is gone, manual choices included, and RA stays.
    - Luciano's page:
      - Tracks shows "Connect Spotify to see their tracks";
      - there is no "You own" and no Spotify button;
      - the gigs are still there.
    - Search `luciano`: only the known card, reading `<K> sets`, with no `on Spotify` cards and no `you own`.
    - Connect again in Settings. The next open of a DJ page fetches its tracks again.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit any fix-ups**

Add only the files you changed, by name. Never `-A`.

```bash
git add <the files you fixed>
git commit -m "fix(dj): <what the check found>

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

Skip this step if nothing needed fixing.
